Purpose:
current artifact payloadsをrewriteせず、canonical retrieval identityをactual physical boundaryへ対応付ける。
This file is not a Trigger Index, not a Coverage Map, not a Regression suite, and not a runtime Schema.
Semantic Trigger content / module procedures are intentionally not duplicated here.

Coordinate convention:
- line numbers: 1-based
- byte offsets: 0-based UTF-8 byte offsets
- end_line_exclusive / end_byte_exclusive: exclusive
- field pointer identifies the actual heading/marker start inside the unchanged Trigger row payload
